# KTM 350 XC-F 2020: aftermarket parts

4950 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 436 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 884 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3630 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
